**E2E legs.** Every one asks the server and ends with a destination-table drop (the standing rule).

- **T1: `e2e_mariadb_cdc.py`, "TRUNCATE with nothing after it", MariaDB→ClickHouse.**
  - (a) Single table: bootstrap 3 rows; `TRUNCATE`; drain.
    - Asks: `SELECT count() FROM ma_trunc_only` = 0.
    - Asks: `SELECT watermark FROM _apitap_state FINAL WHERE dest_table='ma_trunc_only' AND source_id NOT LIKE 'server-identity:%'` changed.
    - A second drain exits 0.
  - (b) Group `tables=[A,B]`: truncate A, insert 5 into B in the same window → A count 0, B count = MariaDB count, both watermarks advanced and equal.
  - (c) `changelog=True`: after truncate-only, `SELECT count() FROM t WHERE _apitap_op='T'` = 1 and `__current` is empty.
  - RED: v0.56.0 raises "missing WAL column list" (dest_ch.rs:1006-1009; the changelog variant at 851-854) and the watermark is unchanged.
- **T2: `e2e_changelog_replay.py`, "a shorter replay appends nothing twice".**
  - Table with `v VARCHAR(40000)`.
  - 100 autocommit updates of ~30 KB, applied once under `APITAP_CDC_WINDOW_BYTES=67108864`.
  - `rewind()`; re-drain under `APITAP_CDC_WINDOW_BYTES=1048576` (min clamp, run.rs:533-538), so the first replay window ends after ~34 ops.
  - Asks ClickHouse:
    - `count() WHERE _apitap_op != 'B'` = 100
    - `count() = uniqExact((_apitap_lsn, _apitap_seq))` over non-B rows
    - the existing `dup_ins` query is empty
    - `__current` digest = MariaDB
  - RED: v0.56.0 gives ≈166 (skip 34 at dest_ch.rs:906, write E34 at :923-926, then re-append).
- **T2b: same file, "a replay window that does not contain the table".**
  - `tables=[U,T]` changelog; one MariaDB transaction burst: 100 U updates of ~30 KB, then 100 small T inserts; apply once.
  - Snapshot and rewind BOTH watermarks; re-drain at 1 MiB, so the first window is U-only.
  - Asks: T's `count() WHERE _apitap_op='I'` = 100, `dup_ins` empty, pairs unique for T and U, both `__current` = source.
  - RED: v0.56.0 duplicates all 100 T inserts via the watermark-only arm.
- **T3: same file, "an older version's rows at this stamp" (rule 1; replaces E's N8 control).**
  - After one applied window with watermark W, plant `INSERT INTO t (id, v, _apitap_op, _apitap_lsn, _apitap_seq, _apitap_at) VALUES (1,'old0','U',W,0,now64(3)),(1,'old1','U',W,1,now64(3)),(1,'old2','U',W,2,now64(3))` and `ALTER TABLE _apitap_cdc_pending DELETE WHERE dest_table='t' SETTINGS mutations_sync=1`.
  - MariaDB `UPDATE t SET v='new' WHERE id=1`; drain.
  - Asks: `SELECT min(_apitap_seq) FROM t WHERE _apitap_lsn = W AND v = 'new'` = 3, and `SELECT v FROM t__current WHERE id=1` = 'new'.
  - RED: v0.56.0 writes seq 0, and `__current` shows `old2`.
- **T3b: same file, "rewound past an attempt".**
  - Rewind the watermark two windows back (snapshot taken two windows earlier); drain.
  - Asserts exit ≠ 0 with "moved backwards", and `count()` is unchanged (asked of ClickHouse).
  - RED: v0.56.0 exits 0 and appends duplicates.
- **T4: `e2e_bq_cdc.py`, "Delete→Insert→Delete on one key".**
  - Bootstrap with id=7; one window `DELETE 7; INSERT 7; DELETE 7`; drain.
  - Asks BigQuery: `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t WHERE id=7` = 0; digest = Postgres; newest `_apitap_state` watermark moved.
  - RED: v0.56.0 fails with "must match at most one source row" and the watermark is unmoved (audit §5).
- **T5: `e2e_toast_rekey.py`, two new cases.**
  - (a) `changelog=True` into ClickHouse: 100 KiB `body`; `UPDATE t SET id=9 WHERE id=1`. Asks `SELECT body FROM t__current WHERE id=9` = source body, `count() … WHERE id=1` = 0, watermark advanced. RED: torn error (changelog.rs:190-196).
  - (b) `DESTS=bq` replica: same update. Asks BigQuery `SELECT LENGTH(body) FROM t WHERE id=9` = 102400 and `COUNT(*) WHERE id=1` = 0. RED: the MERGE ERROR arm (dest_bq.rs:1316-1320) on every retry.
- **T6: new `e2e_state_contract_dests.py`, argv `my|ch|bq|ice`, pg source.**
  - Leg A: `append` bootstraps; insert a sentinel row directly into the destination; `log_based` → exit ≠ 0. Asks the destination: the sentinel is present, `SELECT mode FROM _apitap_state WHERE dest_table='t'` = 'append' (ClickHouse `FINAL`; BigQuery newest row; Iceberg property `apitap.watermark-cursor.<sid>` = 'id').
  - Leg B: `log_based` bootstraps; `append` → exit ≠ 0. Asks: mode still 'log_based' (Iceberg cursor property still `_lsn`), and no `<t>_<tok>__apitap_staging*` exists.
  - Leg C (my/ch/bq): append with cursor=id, then cursor=n → exit ≠ 0.
  - RED:
    - v0.56.0 MySQL leg A re-bootstraps: the sentinel is gone and the row is rewritten as log_based (dest_my.rs:284 → run.rs:990).
    - Leg B proceeds on my/ch/bq/ice.
    - Leg C proceeds on my/ch/bq.
- **T7: `e2e_changelog_bq.py`, "the window's script ran under apitap's job id".**
  - After a window, asks `` SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `region-<loc>`.INFORMATION_SCHEMA.JOBS_BY_USER WHERE job_id LIKE 'apitap_cdc_%' AND creation_time > @t0 AND state='DONE' `` ≥ 1. Uses JOBS_BY_USER, not JOBS_BY_PROJECT (listAll is not granted).
  - RED: v0.56.0 has server-minted ids only.
- **T8: `e2e_changelog_replay.py bq`.** T2 and T2b against `bigquery://` from MariaDB, with the same server assertions in BigQuery SQL (`COUNT(*) = COUNT(DISTINCT FORMAT('%d/%d', _apitap_lsn, _apitap_seq))`). RED as T2/T2b.

**Gate (`benchmarks/gate.py`).**
- Register `("e2e_state_contract_dests.py", "_apitap_state means one thing on every destination", {}, None, ["my"])`, the same with `["ch"]`, `"bq"` with `["bq"]`, and `"iceberg"` with `["ice"]`.
- Register `("e2e_changelog_replay.py", "…on BigQuery", {}, ("bq","mariadb"), ["bq"])`. The capability field accepts a tuple meaning all required; a single-string capability behaves as now.
- The other legs ride existing entries.
- Cluster E's CLAIMS table gains:
  - `mysql-source.truncate-only` → T1
  - `changelog.shorter-replay` → T2
  - `changelog.group-replay` → T2b
  - `changelog.seq-continuation` → T3
  - `changelog.rewind-refused` → T3b
  - `bq.replica-one-row-per-key` → T4
  - `changelog.rekey-toast` → T5a
  - `bq.replica-rekey-toast` → T5b
  - `state.contract-every-engine` → T6
  - `bq.job-identity` → T7
  - `bq.changelog-replay` → T8

**Existing legs that must change:**
- `e2e_changelog_replay.py`: its replay leg stays and now also asserts `count() = uniqExact((lsn,seq))`.
- `e2e_state_contract.py` legs 1/3/4: their grepped phrases are preserved verbatim by `foreign_state_error`, so no edit is needed, but they must stay green.

### 7. Docs

- **docs/design/log_based.md**
  - :115-116: "`deletes: Vec<Key>`, `upserts: Vec<Row>`" → "`deletes: DeleteSet` (each key once, by construction), `upserts`, `residue`; set-image consumers (BigQuery, Iceberg) read `Resolved::rows()`, which yields every touched key exactly once."
  - :127: "`Change { op, row }`" → "`Change { op, row, moved_from }` — the U half of a moved key names the key the row came from, which is where its untouched TOAST cells are read."
  - :137-139: "MySQL carries no TRUNCATE op, so `T` records are Postgres-only." → "Both planes emit `T` (MySQL writes TRUNCATE as a QUERY event; since 0.56.0 it is applied in order, and a window is cut at every DDL so it never spans a layout change)."
  - :145-146: "one window stamps ONE end-LSN" → "one window stamps its START on every row; `_apitap_seq = seq_base + ordinal`, where `seq_base` continues past rows another version wrote at the same stamp. `_apitap_cdc_pending` records `(start, seq_base, end, events)` before the append (ClickHouse) or inside the window's transaction (BigQuery); a replay makes the log at that stamp equal exactly the replayed window — trim, then append."
  - §State (:192-198) add: "Both lanes read `watermark, cursor_col, mode` with no predicate on either, and `naming::state_verdict` decides; a row of the other lane is refused, never invisible."
- **docs/usage.md**
  - :1100: "`_apitap_seq` | order WITHIN the window — …" → "`_apitap_seq` | order within the window's stamp; continues (never restarts) above rows an older version wrote at the same stamp."
  - :1164-1168: replace with "On ClickHouse and BigQuery a replay appends nothing twice: no `(_apitap_lsn, _apitap_seq)` pair is ever written twice and no event appears under two stamps, including a replay that is shorter than the original or omits a table. A replay whose position is behind an earlier append is refused with a message naming the rows to delete."
  - CDC section: add "`append`/`merge` on a CDC-managed table and `log_based` on a cursor-managed table refuse on every destination (Postgres, MySQL, ClickHouse, BigQuery, Iceberg); a cursor switch on an Iceberg table now refuses instead of re-bootstrapping from data."
- **docs/stability.md:23** (destination artifacts): add "`_apitap_cdc_pending` gains `seq_base, end_lsn, events` on ClickHouse (added in place; rows written by 0.56.0 read `events = 0`) and exists on BigQuery from 0.57.0 (clustered, INSERT-only). Rolling back to 0.56.0 after a 0.57.0 run crashed mid-window can re-append that one window with repeated pairs."
- **failure-modes.md:339:** "a same-start replay appends nothing twice" → "a replay of any length appends nothing twice". The lease wording of 335-343 belongs to clusters A and E.
- **CHANGELOG 0.57.0:** §3.4, §3.6, §3.7, §3.11, §3.12 (lost POST / failed poll), §3.13, §3.14 plus the BigQuery replica re-key and the absent-member replay, each with its leg id. Behaviour changes to list:
  - the bulk verdict now runs before the empty-table check;
  - Iceberg and MySQL/ClickHouse/BigQuery cursor switches refuse;
  - the BigQuery NULL CDC watermark errors;
  - MySQL-source keys come from the run's key names, not PRI flags.

### 8. What this does not fix

- **ClickHouse marker merges after a clock step.** `_apitap_cdc_pending` is `ReplacingMergeTree(at)`. A merge after the destination clock steps backward by more than a window's gap can keep an older marker. Reads rank by `(lsn, at)`, but a merged-away row is gone. `_apitap_state` (`ReplacingMergeTree(synced_at)`, dest_ch.rs:129-133) has the same class. Changing either engine needs a table rebuild and is not in this release.
- **A legacy marker naming a stamp that holds only 0.55.x rows.** A 0.56.0 run crashed between its marker and its first INSERT on the very first window after a 0.55.x upgrade, then 0.57.0 ran. The legacy marker names a stamp holding only 0.55.x rows with a clean prefix, and 0.57.0 reads them as that attempt's prefix. That is exactly what 0.56.0 itself would do; the mixed case is refused.
- **Pruning escape on BigQuery.** A 0.55.x group-minimum replay that moved a watermark backward more than 31 days before the 0.57.0 run leaves rows the probe does not see (`PRUNE`). Rule 1 then restarts seq at 0 on that one stamp, if a window happens to start exactly there.
- **One transaction that changes a tracked table's columns between two of its own row changes** is refused (A2), not split.
- **BigQuery:**
  - A genuine server-side double execution of one job id is not defended.
  - A consumer that read trimmed replay rows between two windows sees them again under a new stamp (the documented cost of a stream that cannot seek).
- **Owned elsewhere:**
  - Bootstrap ownership, the double bootstrap and the handover: cluster C.
  - Collector lease close and GC: cluster E (`Released`-gated).
  - ClickHouse TTL, `OWNER_PRED` on an empty row set, the BigQuery fence and its conflict domain, the MySQL TRUNCATE fence, ChangelogTmp tokenization: cluster A.
- **Iceberg** has no changelog, so D does not reach it. Its bulk verdict depends on the cursor property's presence (absent ⇒ Absent, as today).

---

## §2.C Bulk lifecycle: owned pipes, one join loop, one identity per nested run

### 1. Root cause

Bulk workers and loaders have no owner that outlives an error. Four places spawn one task per loader and then run `for t in tasks { t.await?? }`: source/postgres.rs:499-514, source/mysql.rs:1168-1192, source/mysql.rs:1510-1536 and source/clickhouse.rs:575-586. The first `Err` returns and drops the remaining `JoinHandle`s, and on tokio that detaches them. The siblings keep popping from a queue that has no notion of cancellation (`pop`, source/mod.rs:92-105). They keep writing, and they end with `loader.finish()` (postgres.rs:939) while `pipeline::run`'s error arm is already running `sink.discard()` (pipeline/mod.rs:482-495). On the process-wide runtime (py-apitap/src/lib.rs:51-54) they outlive `transfer()`.

The same missing owner sits one level down. The `Loader` contract (sink/mod.rs:59-64) says a worker consumes its loader by `finish` or `abort`, but the worker owns `L` by value. So any of the 19 `loader.send(..).await?` sites, or a panic, drops the loader unconsumed. The 46 `loader.abort(..)` sites (pg 21, my 9, ch 5, csv 2, gh 1, gs 1, gh+api 7) are a convention that nothing enforces.

Worse, dropping is a commit on the three channel-backed loaders:
- The Postgres overlap sender finishes the COPY when its channel closes (sink/postgres.rs:1294-1316).
- MySQL's LOAD DATA sees EOF and commits (sink/mysql.rs:1305-1311).
- ClickHouse commits on a clean end of the request body (sink/clickhouse.rs:2068-2070).
- `BqLoader::abort` itself detaches its poll tasks (`handle.abort()`, sink/bigquery.rs:1491-1493). The server-side load jobs keep running past `discard` (:2210-2222), and `createDisposition: CREATE_IF_NEEDED` (:2035) re-creates the deleted staging.

One level up, the CDC group owns its `__apitap_lock` but hands it to an in-process child run and never takes it back. run.rs:1006-1010 and :1220-1224 release lock and lease so that `transfer(mode=replace)` is not refused by its own parent. Nothing re-owns the table through `bootstrap_finish` (run.rs:1462-1472, dest_pg.rs:228-280).

These are one defect: a child (task, stream, loader, sub-run) whose lifetime is not contained in its owner's.

### 2. The decision

**Mechanism.**
- Sources hold only a `Pipe<L>`, which can `send` but has no `finish` or `abort`.
- The engine is the only consumer: one private `Pipe::drive` per worker and one `JoinSet` join loop in the crate. That loop latches the set's failure before any abort, stops siblings cooperatively at their next `send` or span, and returns only after every task has ended.
- Channel-backed loaders treat "closed without `Finish`" as an abort.
- `BqLoader::abort` waits until every load job it submitted is terminal.
- A CDC bootstrap child mints its own `RunId` that names its parent. `classify` answers `Found::Parent` for the parent's artifacts: never blocking, never deleted. The handover release code is deleted.

**Cost.** One acquire-load per buffer sent (replacing `Counted`'s relaxed add), one `catch_unwind` per worker per table. There are no new knobs and no new dependencies. The failure path now waits for the slowest sibling's current send or span, plus in-flight BigQuery load jobs.

### 3. What changes

**Deviations from the judged design, and why:**

| # | Change | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | The set's cancel flag is a `Latch` with a single-winner `claim()` (CAS), set **before** `abort`. The winning task alone reports `Cause`. | With cancel set before a slow abort (BigQuery), a sibling's `cancelled()` can finish first. "First error in completion order" then reports the wrong error (attack: I6 on the BigQuery/S3 abort path). |
| D2 | `Found::Parent` is a new variant, not `owns()` → `Found::Mine`. | Three sinks delete `Found::Mine` non-lock artifacts: bigquery.rs:1710, mysql.rs:613, clickhouse.rs:654. Under `owns()` the child would delete the parent's marker (attack: `is_self` vs `owns`). A new variant makes every `match` site a compile error until it decides. |
| D3 | `PipeBody::run` returns a `Rows` enum instead of applying `rep > 0 ? rep : own` everywhere. | The ClickHouse source ignores `finish()`'s count today (clickhouse.rs:664-665). I10 must hold by construction, not by argument. |
| D4 | `Pipes::open` aborts already-opened loaders when the k-th `sink.loader()` fails. | Graft. pipeline/mod.rs:467-472 drops them live today. |
| D5 | `sink::FinishMarked` makes drop equal abort on the pg-overlap, MySQL and ClickHouse loaders. | Graft. They commit on drop today (see §1). |
| D6 | No `compile_fail` doctests. | Doctests compile against the crate's **public** API. A `compile_fail` on a `pub(crate)` item fails for the wrong reason and passes vacuously, which would be a fourth worthless green test. A source lint pins the surface instead. |
| D7 | The S3 leg is gated, not residual. | MinIO is already on the rig at 127.0.0.1:9100 under the `iceberg` capability (gate.py:141, e2e_logbased_dests.py:22-23). A BigQuery leg is added under `bq`. |
| D8 | `BqLoader::abort` cancels through `conn.api` and then runs one extra `poll_job`. | A transient poll error in the background task proved nothing terminal. The bulk loader's disposition is at :2035; the design's :778 is the CDC load. |
| D9 | The child in the e2e legs sleeps 12 s after raising. | Process exit kills detached tasks, which would hide the red control. |
| D10 | The bootstrap leg is deterministic: the leg holds `LOCK TABLE _apitap_state IN SHARE MODE`. | No race against a 1-3 s `ALTER`. |
| D11 | Rejected: letting a child with a live parent skip lock peers (attack "bootstrap liveness"). | Unsound under parent eviction. See §8. |

#### 3.1 New file `crates/apitap-core/src/pipe.rs` (`pub(crate) mod pipe;` in lib.rs after `mod progress;`, lib.rs:42)

```rust
use std::{collections::VecDeque, future::Future, panic::AssertUnwindSafe,
          sync::{atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering}, Arc, Mutex}};
use futures::FutureExt as _;
use crate::{error::{Error, Result}, sink::Loader};

/// One set's failure latch. `claim` is its ONLY writer.
pub(crate) struct Latch { failed: AtomicBool }
impl Latch {
    fn new() -> Arc<Self> { Arc::new(Self { failed: AtomicBool::new(false) }) }
    fn is_set(&self) -> bool { self.failed.load(Ordering::Acquire) }
    /// true for exactly ONE caller per set: the task whose error is THE error.
    fn claim(&self) -> bool {
        self.failed.compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::AcqRel, Ordering::Acquire).is_ok()
    }
}

pub(crate) fn cancelled() -> Error {
    Error::Transfer("cancelled: a sibling worker failed first — its error is the one reported".into())
}

/// Replaces WorkQueue/work_queue/pop (source/mod.rs:92-105, DELETED).
#[derive(Clone)]
pub(crate) struct Spans { q: Arc<Mutex<VecDeque<String>>>, latch: Arc<Latch> }
impl Spans {
    fn new(stmts: Vec<String>, latch: Arc<Latch>) -> Self
    /// `while let Some(sql) = spans.next()? {..}` is the only loop shape; it stops by construction.
    pub(crate) fn next(&self) -> Result<Option<String>> {
        if self.latch.is_set() { return Err(cancelled()); }
        Ok(self.q.lock().unwrap().pop_front())
    }
    #[cfg(test)] pub(crate) fn remaining(&self) -> usize
}

/// How a worker's row count is read. Encodes TODAY's per-source rule exactly (I10).
pub(crate) enum Rows {
    FromLoader,           // pg (postgres.rs:939), mysql (:1339, :1469): the finish() value
    Own(u64),             // clickhouse (:664-665): own tally, finish() value ignored
    LoaderElseOwn(u64),   // csv (:340-341), gsheets (:338-339), github+api (:987-988)
}
impl Rows { fn resolve(self, rep: u64) -> u64 { match self {
    Rows::FromLoader => rep, Rows::Own(n) => n, Rows::LoaderElseOwn(n) => if rep > 0 { rep } else { n } } } }

pub(crate) struct Pipe<L: Loader> { inner: Option<L>, latch: Arc<Latch>, counted: bool }
impl<L: Loader> Pipe<L> {
    pub(crate) async fn send(&mut self, buf: Vec<u8>) -> Result<()> {
        if self.latch.is_set() { return Err(cancelled()); }
        if self.counted { crate::progress::add_bytes(buf.len() as u64); }
        self.inner.as_mut().expect("pipe consumed").send(buf).await
    }
    pub(crate) async fn send_framed(&mut self, win: &[u8]) -> Result<(usize, crate::wire::arrowcol::FramedPush)> {
        if self.latch.is_set() { return Err(cancelled()); }
        let r = self.inner.as_mut().expect("pipe consumed").send_framed(win).await;
        if let (true, Ok((n, _))) = (self.counted, &r) { crate::progress::add_bytes(*n as u64); }
        r
    }
    pub(crate) fn reclaim(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<u8>> { self.inner.as_mut()?.reclaim() }
    pub(crate) fn framed_capable(&self) -> bool { self.inner.as_ref().is_some_and(|l| l.framed_capable()) }
    // NO finish, NO abort, NO into_inner. `drive` is MODULE-PRIVATE.

    async fn drive<B: PipeBody<L>>(mut self, body: B, spans: Spans) -> Outcome {
        let r = AssertUnwindSafe(body.run(&mut self, spans)).catch_unwind().await;
        let inner = self.inner.take().expect("a pipe is consumed exactly once");
        match r {
            Ok(Ok(rows)) if !self.latch.is_set() => match inner.finish().await {
                Ok(rep) => Outcome::Done(rows.resolve(rep)),
                Err(e) => if self.latch.claim() { Outcome::Cause(e) } else { Outcome::Cancelled },
            },
            Ok(Ok(_)) => { let _ = inner.abort(cancelled()).await; Outcome::Cancelled }
            Ok(Err(e)) => {
                let first = self.latch.claim();          // BEFORE abort: siblings stop during it (D1)
                let e = inner.abort(e).await;
                if first { Outcome::Cause(e) } else { debug_note(&e); Outcome::Cancelled }
            }
            Err(p) => {
                let first = self.latch.claim();
                let e = inner.abort(Error::Transfer(format!("worker panicked: {}", panic_text(&*p)))).await;
                if first { Outcome::Cause(e) } else { Outcome::Cancelled }
            }
        }
    }
}
impl<L: Loader> Drop for Pipe<L> {   // tripwire only: reachable solely via JoinSet drop on the read path (read.rs:94-98)
    fn drop(&mut self) { if self.inner.is_some() && std::env::var_os("APITAP_DEBUG").is_some() {
        eprintln!("[pipe] dropped undriven — cancelled from above"); } }
}

enum Outcome { Done(u64), Cause(Error), Cancelled }
fn panic_text(p: &(dyn std::any::Any + Send)) -> String  // downcast &str / String, else "non-string payload"
fn debug_note(e: &Error)   // eprintln under APITAP_DEBUG: "[pipe] sibling error after the set failed: {e}"

pub(crate) trait PipeBody<L: Loader>: Send {
    fn run(self, pipe: &mut Pipe<L>, spans: Spans) -> impl Future<Output = Result<Rows>> + Send;
}
pub(crate) trait SpanBody: Send {          // loader-less (MySQL direct-Arrow read lane)
    fn run(self, spans: Spans) -> impl Future<Output = Result<u64>> + Send;
}

async fn drive_span<T: SpanBody>(body: T, spans: Spans, latch: Arc<Latch>) -> Outcome {
    match AssertUnwindSafe(body.run(spans)).catch_unwind().await {
        Ok(Ok(n)) => Outcome::Done(n),
        Ok(Err(e)) => if latch.claim() { Outcome::Cause(e) } else { Outcome::Cancelled },
        Err(p) => if latch.claim() { Outcome::Cause(Error::Transfer(format!("worker panicked: {}", panic_text(&*p)))) } else { Outcome::Cancelled },
    }
}

/// THE join loop. Never returns early.
async fn join_all(mut set: tokio::task::JoinSet<Outcome>, latch: &Latch) -> Result<u64> {
    let (mut cause, mut saw_cancel, mut rows) = (None, false, 0u64);
    while let Some(j) = set.join_next().await {
        match j {
            Ok(Outcome::Done(n)) => rows += n,
            Ok(Outcome::Cause(e)) => cause = Some(e),         // exactly one, by `claim`
            Ok(Outcome::Cancelled) => saw_cancel = true,
            Err(je) => if latch.claim() { cause = Some(Error::Transfer(format!("join: {je}"))) },
        }
    }
    match (cause, saw_cancel) { (Some(e), _) => Err(e), (None, true) => Err(cancelled()), (None, false) => Ok(rows) }
}

pub(crate) async fn run_tasks<T: SpanBody + 'static>(stmts: Vec<String>, bodies: Vec<T>) -> Result<u64> {
    let latch = Latch::new();
    let spans = Spans::new(stmts, latch.clone());
    let mut set = tokio::task::JoinSet::new();
    for b in bodies { set.spawn(drive_span(b, spans.clone(), latch.clone())); }
    join_all(set, &latch).await
}

pub(crate) struct Pipes<L: Loader> { pipes: Vec<Pipe<L>>, latch: Arc<Latch> }   // fields module-private
impl<L: Loader> Pipes<L> {
    /// Bulk lane: open `n` loaders, counted. If the k-th open fails, abort the k-1 already open (D4).
    pub(crate) async fn open<F, Fut>(n: usize, mut open: F) -> Result<Self>
    where F: FnMut() -> Fut, Fut: Future<Output = Result<L>> {
        let latch = Latch::new();
        let mut pipes = Vec::with_capacity(n);
        for _ in 0..n {
            match open().await {
                Ok(l) => pipes.push(Pipe { inner: Some(l), latch: latch.clone(), counted: true }),
                Err(e) => {
                    for mut p in pipes { if let Some(l) = p.inner.take() {
                        let _ = l.abort(Error::Transfer("a sibling pipe failed to open".into())).await; } }
                    return Err(e);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(Self { pipes, latch })
    }
    /// read() lane: ArrowLoaders, NOT counted (read_impl never wrapped them in Counted — I10).
    pub(crate) fn for_read(loaders: Vec<L>) -> Self
    pub(crate) fn len(&self) -> usize
    pub(crate) async fn run<B: PipeBody<L> + 'static>(self, stmts: Vec<String>, mut make: impl FnMut(usize) -> B) -> Result<u64> {
        let Pipes { pipes, latch } = self;
        let spans = Spans::new(stmts, latch.clone());
        let mut set = tokio::task::JoinSet::new();
        for (i, p) in pipes.into_iter().enumerate() { set.spawn(p.drive(make(i), spans.clone())); }
        join_all(set, &latch).await
    }
    /// Single-stream sources: no spawn, SAME drive.
    pub(crate) async fn run_inline<B: PipeBody<L>>(self, stmts: Vec<String>, body: B) -> Result<u64> {
        assert_eq!(self.pipes.len(), 1, "one span always yields one loader");
        let Pipes { pipes, latch } = self;
        let spans = Spans::new(stmts, latch.clone());
        match pipes.into_iter().next().unwrap().drive(body, spans).await {
            Outcome::Done(n) => Ok(n), Outcome::Cause(e) => Err(e), Outcome::Cancelled => Err(cancelled()),
        }
    }
}
```

Toolchain: MSRV is 1.75 (Cargo.toml:11). RPITIT with `+ Send` is the form `Loader` already uses (sink/mod.rs:32). An impl may write `async fn run(..)`, as `impl Loader for Counted` does today. RPITIT captures the `&mut Pipe` lifetime, which is why bodies are structs, not closures. `tokio::task::JoinSet` is in `rt` (Cargo.toml:24). `futures::FutureExt::catch_unwind` is in `futures 0.3` with default `std` (Cargo.toml:27). `Loader: Send + 'static` (sink/mod.rs:23) makes `drive`'s future `'static + Send` when `B: 'static`.

#### 3.2 sink/mod.rs

- **Delete** `Counted<L>` and its impl (:67-107). It is folded into `Pipe::send` and `Pipe::send_framed`.
- Loader doc (:59-64): add "`finish` and `abort` are called only by `pipe::Pipe::drive`. Sources hold `&mut pipe::Pipe<L>`, which has neither."
- **Add** the finish marker used by all three channel loaders:

```rust
pub(crate) enum Msg<B> { Buf(B), Finish }
pub(crate) const DROPPED: &str = "apitap: stream closed without Finish — discarded, never committed";
/// Adapter: `Buf` → Ok(b); `Finish` → clean end; channel closed WITHOUT `Finish` → one Err, then end.
/// Hand-written (not `stream::unfold`) so it is Send + Sync + Unpin for reqwest::Body::wrap_stream
/// and mysql_async InfileData whatever their bounds are.
pub(crate) struct FinishMarked<B> { rx: Option<futures::channel::mpsc::Receiver<Msg<B>>> }
impl<B> FinishMarked<B> { pub(crate) fn new(rx: futures::channel::mpsc::Receiver<Msg<B>>) -> Self }
impl<B> futures::Stream for FinishMarked<B> {
    type Item = std::io::Result<B>;
    fn poll_next(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>) -> Poll<Option<Self::Item>> {
        let Some(rx) = self.rx.as_mut() else { return Poll::Ready(None) };
        match futures::ready!(rx.poll_next_unpin(cx)) {
            Some(Msg::Buf(b)) => Poll::Ready(Some(Ok(b))),
            Some(Msg::Finish) => { self.rx = None; Poll::Ready(None) }
            None => { self.rx = None; Poll::Ready(Some(Err(std::io::Error::other(DROPPED)))) }
        }
    }
}
```

#### 3.3 The three channel loaders: drop is abort (D5)

**Postgres overlap, sink/postgres.rs:1262-1384.**
- `tx` becomes `futures::channel::mpsc::Sender<Msg<Vec<u8>>>`; delete the `Err(())` marker and its doc (:1265-1268).
- In `open` (:1276-1320) the sender task iterates `FinishMarked::new(rx)`:
  - `Ok(buf)` → `copier.send(&buf[..])`, then recycle through `back_tx` as today.
  - `Err(_)` → `let _ = copier.abort("apitap: source failed").await; return Err(Error::Transfer("copy aborted".into()))`.
  - End of stream → `copier.finish()`.
- `send`: `tx.send(Msg::Buf(buf))`. The error arm is unchanged.
- `finish` (Overlap): `if tx.send(Msg::Finish).await.is_err() { return join's error (same match as send) }`, then drop `tx` and await `join`.
- `abort` (Overlap): drop `tx` without `Finish`, then `let _ = join.await`.
- Serial is unchanged. **Implementer check (VPS):** confirm that sqlx 0.8.x `PgCopyIn`'s `Drop` queues a `CopyFail` (grep the registry source for "dropped without calling finish"). If it does not, route Serial through the same sender task with `overlap` semantics minus the back-channel.

**MySQL, sink/mysql.rs.**
- `type Registry = Arc<Mutex<HashMap<u64, InfileData>>>` (:45).
- `register_infile` (:274-283) inserts `Box::pin(rx) as InfileData`. Its body is complete at registration, so close = commit stays correct there.
- The handler (:420-424) passes the boxed stream through.
- `loader()` (:938-946): `mpsc::channel::<Msg<Bytes>>(2)`, registry gets `Box::pin(FinishMarked::new(rx))`. `MySqlLoader.tx: mpsc::Sender<Msg<Bytes>>` (:1290-1295).
- `send`: `Msg::Buf(Bytes::from(buf))`.
- `finish` (:1305-1311): `tx.send(Msg::Finish).await` (map a send error to "mysql LOAD DATA stream closed early"), then `close_channel`, then await `join`.
- `abort` (:1313-1327): `close_channel()` without `Finish` (the adapter yields the `Err`, and LOAD DATA aborts), remove from the registry, await `join`.

**ClickHouse, sink/clickhouse.rs.**
- `spawn_request` (:1989-2002): `channel::<Msg<bytes::Bytes>>(2)`, `reqwest::Body::wrap_stream(FinishMarked::new(rx))`. The `tx` type changes in the struct (:1973-).
- `send` (:2059): `Msg::Buf(bytes::Bytes::from(buf))`.
- `roll` (:2010-2024): `let _ = old_tx.send(Msg::Finish).await; drop(old_tx);` before awaiting `old_join`.
- `finish` (:2068-2075): send `Finish`, drop, await.
- `abort` (:2077-2090): drop `tx` without `Finish`, await `join`. The adapter's `Err` aborts the request, as the existing `Err` item does.

```rust
async fn abort(self, cause: Error) -> Error {
    for p in self.pending_jobs {
        let loc = p.location.as_deref().map(|l| format!("?location={l}")).unwrap_or_default();
        // best effort: jobs.cancel is asynchronous and may not stop a load job
        let _ = self.conn.api(reqwest::Method::POST,
            format!("{BQ_BASE}/projects/{}/jobs/{}/cancel{loc}", self.conn.project, p.id), None).await;
        // MANDATORY: return only once the job is TERMINAL (DONE, succeeded or failed),
        // so discard (:2210-2222) deletes a table no job can re-create (CREATE_IF_NEEDED, :2035).
        let _ = p.handle.await;
        let _ = self.conn.poll_job(&p.id, p.location.as_deref()).await; // 1 GET if DONE; re-polls if the task died on a transient error
    }
    if let Some(uri) = &self.session_uri { /* existing DELETE, :1497-1501, unchanged */ }
    cause
}
```

#### 3.8 Lineage: the bootstrap keeps its lock (naming.rs, dispatch.rs, lib.rs, run.rs)

**Cross-cluster rule (this cluster owns it):** bootstrap ownership is child-token lineage and nothing else.
- Cluster A's `yield_lock`/`retake_lock`, B-surgical's `RunId::child`/`is_mine` plus task-local, and B-structural's parent-token reuse do **not** ship.
- Any code that deletes or sweeps an artifact because it is "this run's" matches `Found::Mine` only. This includes cluster E's `Scan.mine_staging`/`Mine::DeleteStaging` and the sink arms bigquery.rs:1710, mysql.rs:613 and clickhouse.rs:654.
- `Found::Parent` is never deleted, never collected and never blocking.

**naming.rs**
- `RunId { token: String, parent: Option<String> }` (:217-219; derives unchanged). `RunId::mint` (:272) sets `parent: None`.
- Add:

  ```rust
  /// A run spawned INSIDE another run in this process (the CDC bootstrap's full load).
  /// Own token (own staging, own Swap lock); its parent's artifacts classify as `Found::Parent`.
  pub(crate) fn mint_within(kind: LandKind, source_id: &str, parent: &RunId) -> RunId {
      RunId { parent: Some(parent.token.clone()), ..RunId::mint(kind, source_id) }
  }
  pub(crate) fn is_self(&self, t: &str) -> bool { t == self.token }
  pub(crate) fn is_parent(&self, t: &str) -> bool { self.parent.as_deref() == Some(t) }
  ```
